  "complete_environment_observation": "{\n \"app_name\": \"venmo\",\n \"api_name\": \"show_transactions\",\n \"path\": \"/transactions\",\n \"method\": \"GET\",\n \"description\": \"Get a list of your \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"query\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Search query string.\",\n   \"default\": \"\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"user_email\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"If passed, only transactions between you and user with this email address will be shown.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value is email address\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"min_created_at\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Minimum created_at datetime to filter transactions in YYYY-MM-DD format.\",\n   \"default\": \"1500-01-01\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"max_created_at\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Maximum created_at datetime to filter transactions in YYYY-MM-DD format.\",\n   \"default\":   "raw_model_response": "Now I'll set the date range to filter transactions from the start of 2023 to the end of 2023. I'll use the `show_transactions` API with direction 'received' and the specified date range. I'll also use pagination to ensure I get all transactions.\n\nCode:\n```python\nmin_created_at = f\"{current_year}-01-01\"\nmax_created_at = f\"{current_year}-12-31\"\ntransactions = []\npage_index = 0\nwhile True:\n    transaction_page = apis.venmo.show_transactions(\n        access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=f542cec32c125ef1c0417e3dfcceb11c888f2a9f70c2c74265864412c0b7e859>'access_token'],\n        direction='received',\n        min_created_at=min_created_at,\n        max_created_at=max_created_at,\n        page_index=page_index\n    )\n    if not transaction_page:\n        break\n    transactions.extend(transaction_page)\n    page_index += 1\n\ntotal_likes = sum(transaction['like_count'] for transaction in transactions)\nprint(f\"Total likes on received transactions this year: {total_likes}\")\n```",

## Interpretation

- **VERIFIED:** task outcomes, actions, observations, and first divergences are from immutable raw rows.
- **INFERENCE:** N1-N2 differences are consistent with a memory-specific effect of the one-demo-trained whole-bank model.
- **UNVERIFIED:** mechanisms downstream of the first recorded behavioral divergence.
